## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ पवि᳓त्रवत्येव᳓ सं᳓वपति देव᳓स्य त्वा सवितुः᳓ [[प्रसवे᳙ऽश्वि᳓नोर्बा᳓हुभ्यां|प्रसवे᳙ऽश्वि᳓नोर्बाहु᳓भ्यां]] पूष्णो᳓ ह᳓स्ताभ्यां ।  
सं᳓वपामी᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He pours (the ground rice) into (a vessel) what verily contains strainers (purifying blades of sacrificial grass), (chanting), "At the instance of the divine Savitṛ, I pour you with the two arms of the two Aśvins, with the two hands of Pūṣan".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै᳘ पवि᳘त्रवत्येव सं᳘वपति देव᳘स्य त्वा सवितुः᳘ प्रस᳘वेऽश्वि᳘नोर्बा᳘हुभ्यां पूष्णो ह᳘स्ताभ्यां । सं᳘वपामी᳘ति᳘
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सो᳙ ऽसा᳓वेवै᳙त᳓स्य य᳓जुषो ब᳓न्धुर᳓थान्तर्वेदि᳓ वो᳓पविशति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This is, indeed, the explanation (link) of this Yajuṣ (chant).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सो ऽसा᳘वे᳘वैत᳘स्य य᳘जुषो ब᳘न्धुर᳘थान्तर्वेदि वो᳘पविशति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

जघ᳓नेन वा गा᳓र्हपत्यम᳓थोपस᳓र्जनीरे᳓क आ᳓हरति ता᳓ आनीय᳓मानाः पवि᳓त्राभ्यां प्र᳓तिगृह्णाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he sits either inside the altar or behind the Gārhapatya . Then, (some) one brings the mixing- waters. With two strainers he receives them (waters) that are being brought.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

जघ᳘नेन वा गा᳘र्हपत्यम᳘थोपस᳘र्जनीरे᳘क आ᳘हरति ता᳘ आनीय᳘मानाः पवि᳘त्राभ्यां प्र᳘तिगृह्णाति ॥१॥
</details>

## ०२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓त्र जपति स᳓मा᳓प ओ᳓षधीभिरि᳓ति सं᳓ ह्ये᳙त᳓दा᳓प ओ᳓षधिभिरेता᳓भिः पिष्टा᳓भिः सं᳓ग᳓च्छन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then (there) he mutters, "May the waters well (mingle) with the plants!" Indeed the waters thus mingle with the plant viz. with these flours (of rice).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘त्र जपति समा᳘प ओ᳘षधीभिरि᳘ति स᳘ꣳ᳘ ह्येतदा᳘प ओ᳘षधिभिरेता᳘भिः पिष्टा᳘भिः संग᳘च्छन्ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/28 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓मो᳓षधयो र᳓सेने᳓ति सं᳓ह्ये᳙त᳓दो᳓षधयो र᳓सेनैते᳓नाद्भिः᳓ [[पिष्टाः|पिष्टाः᳓]] सं᳓ग᳓च्छन्ते सं᳓ रेव᳓तीर्ज᳓गतीभिरि᳓ति रेव᳓त्य आ᳓पो ज᳓गत्य ओ᳓षधयस्ता᳓ ह्ये᳙त᳓दुभ᳓य्यः सं᳓ग᳓च्छन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

(He says), "May the plants (mingle) with the sap!" Verily thus the plants viz. the flours unite with this sap viz. waters. (He chants), "May the wealthy ones (mix) with those that move!" The wealthy ones are the waters. The moving ones are the plants. These two thus come together.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

समो᳘षधयो र᳘सेने᳘ति स᳘ꣳ᳘ह्येतदो᳘षधयो र᳘सेनैते᳘नाद्भिः᳘ पिष्टाः᳘ संग᳘च्छन्ते स᳘ꣳ᳘ रेव᳘तीर्ज᳘गतीभिरि᳘ति रेव᳘त्य आ᳘पो ज᳘गत्य ओ᳘षधयस्ता᳘ ह्येत᳘दुभ᳘य्यः संग᳘च्छन्ते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

सं᳓ म᳓धुमतीर्म᳓धुमतीभिः पृच्यन्तामि᳓ति सं᳓ र᳓सवत्यो र᳓सवतीभिः पृच्यन्तामि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

## ०३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थ सं᳓यौति ज᳓नयत्यै त्वे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He mixes (them), there, (chanting), "(I mix) you for the sake of generation". He verily mixes (them) thus wherefore it (this preparation of sacrificial offering) will bestow these creatures (progeny) the sacrificer for (his) wealth and (good) food.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ सं᳘यौति ज᳘नयत्यै त्वे᳘ति

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/35 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ य᳓था श्रिये᳙ ऽन्ना᳓द्यायेमाः᳓ प्रजा᳓ य᳓जमानाय य᳓च्छेदेवं᳓ वै᳓ [[त᳓त्सं᳓यौत्य᳓धिवर्त्स्यन्वा᳓|त᳓त्सं᳓यौत्यधिवर्त्स्य᳓न्वा᳓]] एत᳓त्सं᳓यौति य᳓था वा᳓ अ᳓तो᳓ ऽधिवृक्तो जा᳓यते संयु᳓त्य यथादेवतं᳓ वि᳓भजति स᳓ य᳓तो न᳓ संहरिष्य᳓न्भ᳓वती᳓ति वे᳓ति वा॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed he thus mixes (them) in order to put (them) over (the fire) whereby thus it would, after being mixed, become placed (cooked) over (the fire). Either he divides it according to the deities (to whom it has to be offered), because he is not going to gather them (together), or
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स य᳘था श्रि᳘यै ऽन्ना᳘द्यायेमाः᳘ प्रजा य᳘जमानाय य᳘च्छेदेवं वै तत्सं᳘यौत्य᳘धिवर्त्स्यन्वा᳘ एतत्सं᳘यौति य᳘था वा अतो᳘ ऽधिवृक्तो जा᳘यते संयु᳘त्य यथादेवतं वि᳘भजति स य᳘तो न᳘ सꣳहरिष्यन्भ᳘वती᳘ति वे᳘ति वा ॥३॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अय᳓मिह᳓ पुरोळा᳓शम᳓धिश्रयत्यमु᳓त्रै᳓क आ᳓ज्यमर्धो᳓ वा᳓ एष᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य य᳓त्पुरोळा᳓शो᳓ ऽथ य᳓दन्या᳓नि हवीं᳓ष्यर्ध᳓ आ᳓ज्यं स᳓ य᳓श्चाय᳓मस्यार्ध᳓ आत्मा᳓ य᳓श्चासा᳓वि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Puroḍāśa is verily half of the sacrifice; the other oblations are half, that is the ghee.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓त्सहा᳙ग्निं᳓ गमयामे᳓ति त᳓थो आत्मा᳓ यज्ञ᳓स्य सं᳓धीयते॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

"What is this viz. half of the body (of the sacrifice) and what is that yonder—let us make them reach the fire simultaneously"—thus (they think). In that manner verily the body of the sacrifice is joined together.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘त्स᳘हाग्निं᳘ गमयामे᳘ति त᳘थो आत्मा᳘ यज्ञ᳘स्य सं᳘धीयते ॥४॥
</details>

## ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ य᳓ आ᳓ज्यमधिश्र᳓यति स᳓ इषे᳓ त्वे᳓त्य᳓धिश्रयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He who heats the ghee over the fire, he heats it over the fire (chanting), "(I heat) you for sap".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स य आ᳘ज्यमधिश्र᳘यति स᳘ इषे त्वेत्य᳘धिश्रयति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वृ᳓ष्ट्ये त᳓दाह य᳓दिषे᳓ त्वे᳓त्यूर्जे᳓त्वे᳓त्यु᳓द्वासयति यो᳓ वृष्टा᳓दू᳓र्ग्र᳓सो जा᳓यते त᳓स्मा उ एत᳓दाह॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

When he says, "You for sap", he says that for the sake of rain. He removes it saying, "(I remove) you for juice". He says this verily for the sake of that juice, the sap which arises from rain.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

वृ᳘ष्ट्ये त᳘दाह य᳘दिषे त्वे᳘त्यूर्जेत्वेत्यु᳘द्वासयति यो᳘ वृष्टादूर्ग्र᳘सो जा᳘यते त᳘स्मा उ एत᳘दाह ॥५॥
</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थाय᳓म᳓धिवृणक्ति पुरोळा᳓शं घर्मो᳙ऽसि विश्वा᳓युरि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he puts the puroḍāśa on (fire) (chanting), “You are the heat (you are the) life of all”. Thus he makes this verily into a sacrifice.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थायम᳘धिवृणक्ति पुरोळा᳘शं घ᳘र्मोऽसि विश्वा᳘युरि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/37 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

यज्ञ᳓मेवै᳙नं त᳓त्करोति य᳓था घर्मं᳓ प्रवृज्या᳓देवं᳓ प्र᳓वृणक्ति [[विश्वा᳓युरि᳓त्यायुरेवा᳙स्मिं᳓स्त᳓द्दधात्युरु᳓प्रथा|विश्वा᳓युरि᳓त्या᳓युरेवा᳙स्मिं᳓स्त᳓द्दधात्युरु᳓प्रथा]] उरु᳓ प्रथस्वे᳓ति प्रथ᳓यत्येवै᳙नं त᳓दुरु᳓ ते यज्ञ᳓पतिः प्रथतामि᳓ति य᳓जमानायैता᳓माशि᳓षमा᳓शास्ते॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He puts it upon (the fire) as one would put the cauldron on. By “the life of all”, he thus puts verily life into this (sacrificer). (He says), “Oh wide spreading one! (one with wide glory), spread vastly”, (saying this) he verily spreads this (puroḍāśa) thus. He says, “May your master of the sacrifice spread widely (prosper)!”—this prayer for blessing he seeks for the sacrificer.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यज्ञ᳘मे᳘वैनं त᳘त्करोति य᳘था घर्मं᳘ प्रवृज्या᳘देवं प्र᳘वृणक्ति विश्वा᳘युरित्यायुरे᳘वास्मिꣳस्त᳘द्दधात्युरु᳘प्रथा उरु᳘ प्रथस्वे᳘ति प्रथ᳘यत्ये᳘वैनं त᳘दुरु᳘ ते यज्ञ᳘पतिः प्रथतामि᳘ति य᳘जमानायैता᳘माशि᳘षमा᳘शास्ते ॥६॥
</details>

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तं᳓ न᳓ सत्रा᳓ पृथुं᳓ कुर्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He should not make it too (absolutely) wide. If he should make it too wide, he would be making it human.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तं न᳘ सत्रा᳘ पृथुं᳘ कुर्यात्
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः)</summary>

- कुर्यात् (तिङन्तम् अनुदात्तम्, पा॰ ८.१.२८, *TS*-23, *TB*-13, *KS*-6, *MS*-41)
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/32 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[मानुषंह|मानुषं᳓ह]] कुर्याद्य᳓त्सत्रा᳓ पृथुं᳓ कुर्या᳓द्युद्धं᳓ वै᳓ त᳓द्यज्ञ᳓स्य य᳓न्मानुष᳓मश्वशफमात्रं᳓ कुर्या᳓दि᳓त्याहुः᳓ क᳓स्त᳓द्वेद या᳓वानश्वशफो᳓ य᳓थैव᳓ म᳓न्येत न᳓ सत्रा᳓ पृथु᳓मिव त᳓थैव᳓ कुर्यात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

What is human is ruinous for the sacrificer. They (some) say, “He should make it of the size of a horse-hoof”. Who knows how much is the horse-hoof ? He may make it verily that way as he thinks it is not too wide.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

मानुषꣳह कुर्याद्य᳘त्सत्रा᳘ पृथुं᳘ कुर्या᳘द्युद्धं वै त᳘द्यज्ञ᳘स्य य᳘न्मानुष᳘मश्वशफमात्रं᳘ कुर्यादि᳘त्याहुः कस्त᳘द्वेद या᳘वानश्वशफो य᳘थैव म᳘न्येत न᳘ सत्रा᳘ पृथु᳘मिव त᳘थैव᳘ कुर्यात् ॥७॥
</details>

## ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/36 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓मद्भि᳓रभि᳓मृशति सकृ᳓द्वा त्रि᳓र्वा [[य᳓देवा᳙स्यावघ्न᳓न्तो|य᳓देवा᳙स्या᳓वघ्न᳓न्तो]] वा पिंष᳓न्तो वोलूखलमुसले᳓न वा दृषदुपले᳓न वा वि᳓ वा वृह᳓न्ति क्षिण्व᳓न्ति वा᳓पो वै᳓ भेषजं᳓ शा᳓न्तिस्त᳓दद्भिः᳓ शा᳓न्त्या शमयति त᳓दद्भिः᳓ सं᳓दधाति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He touches it with water, once or thrice, for whatever of this (rice-offering) they either wear out or hurt, threshing with the pestle and mortar or grinding with the two millstones. Verily the waters are medicine, abatement (of evil). Therefore he removes it (that hurt) with the expiation, viz. water. Thus (therefore) he writes it (makes good) with water.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘मद्भि᳘रभि᳘मृशति सकृ᳘द्वा त्रि᳘र्वा य᳘दे᳘वास्यावघ्न᳘न्तो वा पिꣳष᳘न्तो वोलूखलमुसले᳘न वा दृषदुपले᳘न वा वि᳘ वा वृह᳘न्ति क्षिण्व᳘न्ति वा᳘पो वै᳘ भेषजꣳ शा᳘न्तिस्त᳘दद्भिः शा᳘न्त्या शमयति त᳘दद्भिः सं᳘दधाति᳘॥८॥
</details>

## ०९

सो᳙ऽभि᳓मृशत्य᳓ग्नि᳓ष्टे त्व᳓चं मा᳓ हिंसीदि᳓ति ।
</details>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/01/02/2/36_so.abhimRshati_agniShTe.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

12. He touches it over, with the text (Vāj. S. I, 22): 'May the fire not injure thy skin!'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He touches it, saying, “May Agni not hurt your skin?” For he is going to heat it (thus) now, (he is) going to cook (it).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

सो ऽभि᳘मृशति॥  
(त्य) अग्नि᳘ष्टे त्व᳘चं मा᳘ हिᳫँ᳭ सीदि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

सो ऽभि᳘मृशति॥  
अग्नि᳘ष्टे त्व᳘चम् मा᳘ हिᳫं सीदि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

सो ऽभिमृशति- **”अग्निष्टे त्वचं मा हिँसीत्”** -(१ अ० २२ मं०) इति ।
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थैनं प᳓र्यग्नि करोत्य᳓च्छिद्रमेवै᳙नमेत᳓त्समन्त᳓मग्नि᳓ना प᳓रिगृह्णाति ने᳓देनं नाष्ट्रा᳓ र᳓क्षांसि प्रमृशा᳓नि᳓त्यग्नि᳓र्हि᳓ नाष्ट्रा᳓णां र᳓क्षसामपहन्ता᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he makes it skirted with fire (he takes a firebrand round it in pradakṣiṇā way). Thus he encloses this, thus, continuously without a gap. With fire, lest the destructive Rakṣasas, should touch it. Indeed Agni is the repeller of the Rakṣasas.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थैनं प᳘र्यग्नि करोत्य᳘च्छिद्रमे᳘वैनमेत᳘त्समन्त᳘मग्नि᳘ना प᳘रिगृह्णाति ने᳘देनं नाष्ट्रा र᳘क्षाꣳसि प्रमृशानि᳘त्यग्निर्हि᳘ नाष्ट्रा᳘णाꣳ र᳘क्षसामपहन्ता॥१०॥
</details>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थो᳓ल्मुकेनाभि᳓तपति देव᳓स्त्वा सविता᳓ श्रपयतु व᳓र्षिष्ठे᳓ ऽधि ना᳓क इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He heats it with a firebrand (chanting), “May the divine Savitṛ, cook you in the highest heaven”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अथो᳘ल्मुकेनाभि᳘तपति देव᳘स्त्वा सविता᳘ श्रपयतु व᳘र्षिष्ठे᳘ ऽधि ना᳘क इ᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

न᳓ वा᳓ एत᳓स्य मनुष्यः᳙ श्रपयिता᳓ त᳓देनं देव᳓ एव᳓ सविता᳓ श्रपयतु ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Man is not, indeed, the one who bakes (cooks) it. Therefore (he says), “May the divine Savitṛ himself cook this”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

न वा᳘ एत᳘स्य मनु᳘ष्यः श्रपयिता त᳘देनं देव᳘ एव᳘ सविता᳘ श्रपयतु
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व᳓र्षिष्ठे᳓ ऽधि ना᳓क इ᳓ति देवत्रो᳙ एत᳓दाहा᳓थाभि᳓मृशति शृतं᳓ वेदानी᳓ति॥

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थाभि᳓वासयति ने᳓न्नग्न᳓ इव मुषितः᳓ श᳓याता᳓ इ᳓ति ने᳓देनमुप᳓रिष्टान्नाष्ट्रा᳓ र᳓क्षांस्यवप᳓श्यानी᳓ति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

17. He covers it over, with the text (Vāj. S. I, 23): 'May the sacrifice not be liable to languish, nor the sacrificer's race liable to languish!'

He covers it (thinking), “May the sacrifice not be (prone to be) languied and may the progeny (offspring) of the sacrificer not be (prone to be) languied”.

</details>

## १६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ते᳓ हे᳓न्द्रम᳓नुचेरुर्य᳓थैत᳓र्ह्य᳓पि ब्राह्मणः᳓ क्षत्रि᳓यमाशं᳓समानो ऽनुच᳓रति स᳓ य᳓त्र विश्व᳓रूपं त्वाष्ट्र᳓मि᳓न्द्रो जघा᳓न त᳓स्य ह व᳓ध्यस्य विदां᳓ चक्रुः᳓ श᳓श्वद्धैनं त्रित᳓ एव᳓ जघान ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They verily followed Indra just as even now a Brahmin follows a Kṣatriya blessing (praising) (him). Where (when) Indra killed Viśvarūpa, the son of Tvaṣṭṛ, they knew indeed of his going to be killed. Verily Trita himself quickly killed him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते हे᳘न्द्रम᳘नुचेरुर्य᳘थैतर्ह्य᳘पि ब्राम्हणः᳘ क्षत्रि᳘यमाश᳘ꣳ᳘समानो ऽनुच᳘रति स य᳘त्र विश्व᳘रुपं त्वाष्ट्रमि᳘न्द्रो जघा᳘न त᳘स्य ह व᳘ध्यस्य विदां᳘ चक्रुः श᳘श्वद्धैनं त्रित᳘ एव᳘ जघान
</details>

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓थोचुर᳓पैवे᳙म᳓ ए᳓नो गच्छन्त्वि᳓ति यो᳙ ऽस्य᳓ व᳓ध्यस्या᳓वेदिषुरि᳓ति त᳓थे᳓ति होचुर्यज्ञ᳓ एवै᳙षु मृष्टामि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then they (the people) said, “May these (Āptyas) themselves, who had known of this person (Viśvarūpa) going to be killed, get to sin”. They said, “So be it”. “Let the sacrifice itself wipe it (clean it) upon these (Āptyas)”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थोचुर᳘पै᳘वेम ए᳘नो गच्छन्त्वि᳘ति᳘ यो ऽस्य व᳘ध्यस्या᳘वेदिषुरि᳘ति तथे᳘ति होचुर्यज्ञ᳘ ए᳘वैषु मृष्टामि᳘ति
</details>

त᳓देष्वेत᳓द्यज्ञो᳓ मृष्टे᳓ य᳓देभ्यो ऽङ्गुलिप्रणे᳓जनं पात्रीनिर्णे᳓जनं निन᳓यन्ति॥

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

## १८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓ उ हैत᳓ ऊचुर᳓त्येवे᳙द᳓मस्म᳓त्परो᳓ नयामे᳓ति कि᳓मभी᳓ति य᳓ ए᳓वादक्षिणे᳓न यज्ञे᳓न य᳓जाता इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

These (Āptyas) then verily said, “Let us lead it (make it go) past beyond us”. “Towards whom?” “Verily (towards him) who may perform a sacrifice without (paying to the priests) any fee”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘ उ हैत᳘ ऊचुर᳘त्ये᳘वेद᳘मस्म᳘त्परो᳘ नयामे᳘ति कि᳘मभी᳘ति य᳘ ए᳘वादक्षिणे᳘न यज्ञे᳘न य᳘जाता इ᳘ति
</details>

## १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓त एतां᳓ देवा᳓ दर्शपूर्णमास᳓योर्द᳓क्षिणामकल्पयन्य᳓दन्वाहार्यं᳙ ने᳓ददक्षिणं᳓ हवि᳓र᳓सदि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore, the gods fixed this fee, in the new moon and full moon sacrifices, viz. the anvāhārya (remainder of rice-grains, cooked over the Dakṣiṇāgni and meant for consumption by the priests). lest the offering should be without a fee.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘त एतां᳘ देवा᳘ दर्शपूर्णमास᳘योर्द᳘क्षिणामकल्पयन्य᳘दन्वाहा᳘र्यं ने᳘ददक्षिण᳘ꣳ᳘ हविर᳘सदि᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓देभ्यो ना᳓ना नि᳓न्यति᳓ त᳓थैभ्यो᳓ ऽसमदं करो᳓त्यु᳓ल्मुकेनाभि᳓तपति शृत᳓मेनानैच्छा᳓दि᳓ति प्रत्य᳓ङ् नि᳓नयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

There- fore he pours out the water with which the fingers and the vessels have been washed, in the sacrifice (to the Āptyas) individually. Thus he brings about absence of conflict between them.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

प्रा᳓ग्वै᳓ यज्ञः᳓ सं᳓तिष्ठते ने᳓द्यज्ञ᳓मनुसं᳓ति᳓ष्ठाता इ᳓ति॥

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He heats it (that water) with a fire brand, so that it would reach them cooked. He pours (facing) towards the west-verily the sacrifice -remains eastward-(he pours the water towards the west). Lest he should be making (this) a sacrifice.
</details>

## २०

<div class="js_include" includetitle="false" newlevelforh1="3" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarva-prastutiH/01/02/3/17_sa_ninayati_tritAya.md">
<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ नि᳓नयति — त्रिता᳓य त्वा द्विता᳓य त्वैकता᳓य त्वे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

He pours it out with the formulas, 'For Trita thee!' 'For Dvita thee!' 'For Ekata thee!'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He pours out, saying, "For Trita (I pour) you" "For Dvita (I pour) you" "For Ekata (I pour) you".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स नि᳘नयति त्रिता᳘य त्वा द्विता᳘य त्वैकता᳘य त्वे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - Weber</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - विस्वरम्</summary>

स निनयति- **”त्रिताय त्वा द्विताय त्वैकताय त्वा”**- (१ अ० २३ मं०) इति ।
</details>

पशु᳓र्ह वा᳓ एष᳓ आ᳓लभ्यते य᳓त्पुरोळा᳓शस्ते᳓ ह देवाः᳓ पु᳓रुषमेवा᳓ग्रे पशु᳓मा᳓लभिरे त᳓स्या᳓लब्धस्य मे᳓धो᳓ ऽपचक्राम ।

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<div class="js_include collapsed" newlevelforh1="3" title="सर्वाष् टीकाः" unfilled url="/vedAH_yajuH/vAjasaneyam/mAdhyandinam/shatapatha-brAhmaNam/brAhmaNam/sarvASh_TIkAH/01/02/3/28_sa_yAvadvIryavaddha_vA.md">
<details><summary><h3>सर्वाष् टीकाः ...{Loading}...</h3></summary>
<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

They found it (in the shape of) those two (substances), the rice and barley: therefore even now they obtain those two by digging; and as much

efficacy as all those sacrificed animal victims would have for him, so much efficacy has this oblation (of rice &c.) for him who knows this. And thus there is in this oblation also that completeness which they call 'the fivefold animal sacrifice.'
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

As much efficacy as all these sacrificial victims that are tied up (killed and offered) will have, so much efficacy (vigour) indeed will this oblation of his have.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - श्रीधरादि</summary>

स या᳘वद्वीर्यवद्ध वा᳘ ऽअस्यैते स᳘र्व्वे पश᳘व आ᳘लब्धाः स्युः
</details>

<details><summary>सायणः</summary>

एतज्ज्ञानस्य फलमाह- **स यावदि**ति । स इति पूर्ववद् [^१_६४] बह्वर्थे- एकवचनम् । 'अस्य’ यजमानस्य ते ’एते’ पुरुषादयः सर्वे पशव आलब्धाः सन्तो यत्परिमाणविशिष्टवीर्यवद् हविः ’स्युः’ भवेयुः ’अस्य’ व्रीहियवयोः सर्वपशुसारत्वं विदुषो यजमानस्य तन्निष्पादितं पुरोडाशादिकं ’हविः’ तावद्वीर्यवद् ’हविरेव’ भवति । एवं सर्वपशुसारत्वात् पुरोडाशः पशुरूप एव संस्क्रियत इत्यर्थः ।

[^१_६४]: व्यत्ययेनेति यावत् । यथा प्रवृतवानित्यत्र प्रावृणत तथेत्यर्थः ।
</details>
</details>
</div>

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अ᳓त्र वै᳓ सा᳓ संप᳓द्य᳓दाहुः᳓ पा᳓ङ्क्तः पशु᳓रि᳓ति यदै᳙व᳓ पिष्टा᳓नि भ᳓वन्त्य᳓थ लो᳓मानि भवन्ति यदै᳙वा᳙प᳓ आन᳓यत्य᳓थ त्व᳓ग्भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Here indeed is that wealth about which they say, "The animal (to be offered in the sacrifice) is five-fold". Even when the rice-flours come to be, then there are the hair. Even when he brings the waters, then the skin comes to be.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘त्र वै सा᳘ संपद्य᳘दाहुः पा᳘ङ्क्तः पशुरि᳘ति य᳘दैव᳘ पिष्टा᳘नि भ᳘वन्त्य᳘थ लो᳘मानि भवन्ति य᳘दै᳘वाप᳘ आन᳘यत्य᳘थ त्व᳘ग्भवति
</details>

## २२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एषो᳙ सा᳓ संप᳓त्स᳓ यं᳓ पु᳓रुषमा᳓लभन्त स᳓ किंपुरुषो᳙ ऽभवत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This is indeed that wealth. That man (person) whom they had tied up (had offered) (as sacrificial victim), became kimpuruṣa (a despicable man).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ए᳘षो सा᳘ संपत्स यं पु᳘रुषमा᳘लभन्त स᳘ किंपुरु᳘षो ऽभवत्
</details>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

<details><summary>Eggeling</summary>

The sheep which they had sacrificed, became a camel. The goat which they had sacrificed, became a śarabha [^egg_180].

[^egg_180]: 52:1 A fabulous kind of deer with eight legs, which was supposed to kill elephants and lions.

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The goat which they had offered, that became a Sarabha, (a strange animal with eight legs, capable of killing even the lion).
</details>
